Result: 模型在多数提示对上行为一致（63.7%），但内部存在显著风险区分信号；模型虽接受预设错误，但其潜在层保留真实信息；手术切除错误方向可显著恢复正确回答（11/25，p=0.0010）；线性探测虽有效，但正交消融无法恢复性能，表明线性可解性不蕴含因果控制。

Conclusion: 行为评估与内部读取在多数情况下系统性不一致，线性可解性不能保证对输出的因果控制。内省学框架为理解模型内部知识与行为表现的脱节提供了理论与方法基础。

Abstract: Evaluating an aligned language model by reading its answers assumes the answers carry the distinction the evaluator cares about. We introduce LLM endognostics, a white-box internal auditing framework designed to extract and causally manipulate latent knowledge within the residual stream. Applied to Minerva-7B-Instruct-v1.0 on 124 minimal prompt pairs over 12 categories of professional risk, behavioral evaluation fails on most of the set: the model acts identically on 63.7% of the pairs (95% CI [55.0%, 71.6%]), complying with or refusing both members. Yet, projecting the residual stream onto the vocabulary by a Jacobian lens reveals a statistically significant Contrastive Endognostic Margin, proving the model maintains robust risk differentiation internally. In a second protocol crossing 25 facts with five linguistic framings, we show that the model conforms to presupposed falsehoods in 72% of cases, despite representing the true entity in its latent layers. Surgically ablating the direction of the planted falsehood restores the correct answer in 11 of 25 suppressed cases (McNemar p = 0.0010), validated by blind human annotation (binary agreement kappa = 0.68). In contrast, an out-of-sample linear probe achieves 77% accuracy at layer 10, but its orthogonal ablation yields a 0% recovery rate. This establishes a fundamental theoretical dissociation: abstract linear representation does not imply causal control over verbalization. Our main contribution is the formalization of endognostics to prove that behavioral evaluation and internal reading systematically disagree in the common case, and that linear decodability is decoupled from causal control.

</details>


### [101] [Team DArgk at the 2026 ELOQUENT lab for evaluating generative language model quality: Residuals of Humanity: AI Detection Evasion via GRPO Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22221)
*Antonela Tommasel,Juan Manuel Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHADE 是一种基于强化学习的对抗性文本生成框架，通过优化策略以逃避检测器。它使用基于PAN 2025 mdok系统的替代检测器反馈，对LLaMA模型进行微调。实验表明，全量微调结合小KL正则化可实现98.5%的欺骗率，远超基础模型的1.5%，而LoRA方法在正则化下效果较差。语言学分析显示，成功规避与更短、更简单、词汇多样性更低的输出相关，说明高欺骗性并不等同于更像人类写作。在正式的Voight-Kampff竞赛中，其提交排名第六和第七，表明针对单一检测器的优化难以泛化到未知分类器。研究揭示了强化学习在对抗性文本生成中的潜力与局限，并呼吁采用更鲁棒的多检测器评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的文本越来越接近人类写作，促使开发自动检测工具。然而，现有检测器在面对对抗性生成时的鲁棒性尚不明确，亟需研究如何有效规避检测，同时评估检测系统的可靠性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出SHADE框架，将检测规避建模为策略优化问题，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）对指令微调的LLaMA模型进行训练，利用基于PAN 2025 mdok系统的替代检测器提供反馈信号。对比全量微调与LoRA微调在不同正则化条件下的表现。

Result: 全量微调在小KL正则化下实现98.5%的替代检测器规避率，显著优于基础模型的1.5%；LoRA方法效果较差。语言分析发现规避成功的文本具有更短、更简单、词汇多样性更低的特点。在真实竞赛中，系统排名第六和第七，表明单检测器优化难以泛化至未知检测器。

Conclusion: 强化学习可用于有效生成对抗性文本以规避检测，但高规避率未必带来更高的人类相似性。当前基于单一检测器的评估存在局限，应发展多检测器、更鲁棒的评估机制以提升检测系统的可靠性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and coherent text that is increasingly difficult to distinguish from human writing, motivating the development of automatic AI-generated text detectors. However, the robustness of such detectors under adversarial generation remains uncertain. This paper presents SHADE (Stochastic Human-like generation via Adversarial Detector Evasion), a reinforcement learning framework that formulates detector evasion as a policy optimization problem. Instead of applying post-hoc perturbations or prompting-based rewriting, SHADE fine-tunes an instruction-tuned LLaMA model with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), using feedback from a surrogate detector based on the PAN 2025 mdok system. Our experiments show that full fine-tuning with a small KL regularization penalty achieves $98.5\%$ surrogate evasion, compared to $1.5\%$ for the base model, while LoRA-based adaptation is substantially less effective under regularization. Linguistic analysis reveals that successful evasion is associated with shorter, simpler, and less lexically diverse outputs, suggesting that high detector evasion does not necessarily correspond to more human-like writing. In the official Voight-Kampff competition setting, our submissions ranked sixth and seventh, indicating that optimization against a single surrogate detector only partially transfers to unseen evaluation classifiers. These results highlight both the potential and limitations of reinforcement learning for adversarial AI-text generation and motivate more robust, multi-detector evaluation protocols for AI-generated text detection.

</details>


### [102] [EAVer: Long-Form Factuality Verification as an End-to-End Agentic Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22223)
*Kening Zheng,Aoying Zheng,Zhigang Chang,Yazhi Guo,Miaotian Guo,Qingwei Zong,Xianhai Xie,Weiqiang Jin,Chengze Li,Hanrong Zhang,Jie Yang,Wei-Chieh Huang,Lingzhe Zhang,Liancheng Fang,Xin Zou,Hanqian Li,Jiahao Huo,Yibo Yan,Zizhuang Deng,Lei Miao,Wei Guo,Haihong Tang,Bo Zheng,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EAVer是一种端到端的代理验证器，通过学习统一策略来控制整个响应级别的验证流程，将语义相关的声明分组，根据置信度决定直接验证或目标搜索，并将搜索返回的证据以紧凑的上下文记忆形式保留，供跨声明复用。为训练该策略，提出了一种特权教师合成管道，将真实声明标注转换为包含实时搜索的多轮工具交互轨迹。通过结构、标签对齐、工具使用、搜索预算和泄漏检查，生成了1,447条高质量轨迹，并构建了794对双向同轨迹偏好数据，支持基于事实决策标记的直接偏好优化（DPO）。实验表明，EAVer在多个基准测试中均显著优于现有基线，尤其在VeriFastScore上提升2.88点，FaStFact-Bench上提升4.73点，且搜索次数减少约80%。同时，在4B至32B参数规模模型上均表现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长格式事实验证采用静态分解-搜索-验证流水线，独立处理每个声明导致计算资源随声明数量线性增长，并重复搜索重叠证据。为解决效率与冗余问题，需一种能统筹全局、智能调度验证流程的系统。

Method: 提出EAVer，一个端到端的代理验证框架，通过学习统一策略实现声明分组、动态路由验证/搜索、以及跨声明证据复用；设计特权教师合成机制生成带实时搜索的多轮交互轨迹；构建双向同轨迹偏好对用于决策导向的直接偏好优化（DPO）。

Result: EAVer在VeriFastScore上比最强基线高2.88点Macro-F1，FaStFact-Bench上高4.73点，搜索调用量减少约80%；在不同规模模型（4B–32B）上均表现优异，具备强泛化能力。

Conclusion: EAVer通过统一策略与智能证据管理，显著提升长格式事实验证的效率与准确性，同时大幅降低对外部搜索的依赖，是高效、可扩展的事实验证新范式。

Abstract: Long-form factuality verification is commonly implemented as a static decompose-search-verify pipeline, with separately prompted modules processing claims and invoking external search. Treating claims independently makes LLM and search calls scale with claim count and causes repeated searches for overlapping evidence about related claims. We introduce EAVer, an End-to-end Agentic Verifier that learns to control the complete response-level verification workflow as a unified policy. EAVer groups semantically related claims, routes each group to direct verification or targeted search based on confidence, and keeps evidence returned by search in compact in-context memos for cross-claim reuse. To train this policy, we develop a privileged-teacher synthesis pipeline that converts gold claim annotations into executable multi-turn tool-interaction trajectories with live search rather than post-hoc rationales. Structural, label-alignment, tool-use, search-budget, and leakage checks yield 1,447 quality-controlled trajectories. We further construct 794 bidirectional same-trajectory preference pairs that keep claim grouping, search, and evidence fixed, enabling decision-focused Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) over factuality-decision tokens. The results with Qwen3-8B show that EAVer outperforms the strongest search-based baseline on each benchmark by 2.88 Macro-F1 points on VeriFastScore and 4.73 points on the out-of-distribution FaStFact-Bench, while using about 80% fewer searches than the most search-efficient baseline. Moreover, EAVer consistently improves performance across models ranging from 4B to 32B parameters, demonstrating its strong generalizability.

</details>


### [103] [From Trait Vectors to Circuits: Tracing Refusal and Sycophancy Through Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22224)
*Oscar Miró López-Feliu,Maya Ozbayoglu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在激活空间中通过操纵来改变安全相关行为的方向，是否与模型自身产生该行为所用的路径一致。针对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型中的拒绝行为和奉承行为，研究通过提取并验证过的特征向量，将计算过程分为重构电路（前向）和传输电路（后向），测试恢复该坐标能否恢复因消融而丢失的行为。对于拒绝行为，两个电路均紧凑且忠实，仅恢复坐标即可几乎完全恢复被消除的拒绝信号，且基于该向量构建的电路在边数约为直接输入输出电路一半的情况下，保持了相当的忠实度。而对于奉承行为，传输电路紧凑但重构电路更广泛且部分忠实。研究结果表明，引导干预的传输电路并不自动等同于产生该行为的原始计算路径，并对每个电路进行了反事实、目标和行为测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在激活空间中通过方向操纵改变模型行为时，这种操纵是否通过模型自身的计算路径，还是通过另一组组件实现。特别关注拒绝和奉承这两种已被验证的安全相关行为，以理解其内在机制是否可被外部干预有效重建。

Method: 使用已提取并验证的特质向量将模型计算流程分解为前向的重构电路和后向的传输电路；通过消融实验移除特定行为信号，再尝试通过恢复向量坐标来检验行为是否可恢复；评估各电路的紧凑性与忠实度，并对比其与直接输入输出路径的表现。

Result: 对于拒绝行为，重构与传输电路均紧凑且高度忠实，恢复坐标即能基本复现原行为；对于奉承行为，传输电路紧凑但重构电路较宽泛且仅部分忠实；表明引导干预路径与模型原生行为生成路径不一致，需分别进行反事实、目标及行为测试验证。

Conclusion: 导向干预的传输路径并非总是对应于模型自身产生行为的计算路径，二者存在差异。因此，仅依赖传输路径无法充分解释行为生成机制，必须结合重构与传输两方面进行系统分析，才能准确理解模型行为的内在机制。

Abstract: A direction in activation space that changes safety-relevant behavior when steered is not necessarily one the model uses to produce that behavior on its own. We therefore ask whether steering acts through the computation of the unmodified model or through a different set of components, studying two traits whose directions have been extracted and validated in prior work: refusal and sycophancy in Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. For each, we use the trait vector to split the computation into a reconstruction circuit before the vector and a transmission circuit after it. We then test whether restoring the coordinate returns behavior removed by ablation. For refusal, both circuits are compact and faithful, restoring the coordinate alone recovers almost all of the refusal signal lost to ablation, and a circuit built around the vector matches the faithfulness of a direct input-to-output circuit at roughly half the edges. For sycophancy, transmission is compact, but reconstruction is broader and only partially faithful. Circuits that transmit a steering intervention are therefore not automatically the circuits that produce the behavior, and we report the counterfactual, target, and behavioral test for every circuit accordingly.

</details>


### [104] [Do LLMs Choose Like Humans? Using Cognitive Theory to Evaluate LLM Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22225)
*Johnathan Sun,Andrei Shleifer,Yonatan Belinkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在决策行为上是否与人类有相似的内在机制。通过一个包含14万次试验的产品选择基准测试，发现尽管LLMs表现出类似人类的上下文敏感性，但在特征关注度（如价格与质量）的重新加权方面并未表现出一致性。无论模型规模或思维链推理如何，都无法有效减少上下文敏感性或生成类人行为。结论是：LLM的决策机制与人类存在本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型的决策行为是否反映与人类相似的深层认知机制，还是仅停留在表面模仿。

Method: 在14万次试验的新型产品选择基准上，评估12个开源及商业大语言模型的上下文敏感性，分析其在问题分类和注意力分配上的表现，并比较不同规模与思维链推理对行为的影响。

Result: LLMs表现出人类类似的上下文敏感性和问题分类变化，但未稳定地重新分配对价格与质量等特征的注意力；模型规模和链式思维推理均未能显著减弱上下文敏感性或产生类人行为。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的决策机制与人类认知机制存在本质区别，其行为更多体现为表面层面的模仿而非深层认知一致。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit a range of human-like decision-making behaviors, but whether these reflect similar underlying mechanisms or surface-level mimicry remains unclear. We evaluate whether LLM context sensitivity aligns with a cognitive economic theory that explains human behavior through problem categorization and attention allocation. Across 12 open-source and commercial LLMs on a novel 140,000-trial product choice benchmark, context induces human-like shifts in choice and problem categorization, but does not reliably reweight attention between features like price and quality. Neither scale nor chain-of-thought reasoning reliably attenuates context sensitivity or generates human-like behavior. These results suggest that LLM decision mechanisms are distinct from human ones.

</details>


### [105] [Swiss-Knife: A Framework for Reconfigurable Externalised Multi-Objective Alignment at Decode Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22226)
*Agnibh Karmakar,Mayur Parvatikar,Shreyash Dhoot,Amit Dhanda,Aman Chadha,Kapil Wanaskar,Vinija Jain,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Swiss-Knife框架，实现解码时外部多目标对齐，通过可热插拔的评分刀片、批量归一化、成对聚合算子和选择规则，实现灵活、稳定且高效的对齐。其核心是满足六条公理的成对聚合算子，具有统一数学形式，并证明了成对聚合在对抗性奖励污染下具有Lipschitz稳定性，而argmax则不具备。引入候选-批量归一化（CBN）使权重单纯形对奖励模型的尺度不变性得以保持。实验表明，基于DPO-LoRA的实例在帮助性、诚实性和无害性平衡上优于现有六种方法（调和F1 0.797 vs. 0.750，p < 1e-14），拒绝率最低，响应速度仅需0.05毫秒，无需梯度计算。消融实验验证了CBN的重要性，未使用时损失0.217 F1，且数据支持预测机制：低方差评分器影响力从33%降至9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前解码时对齐方法存在设计单一、缺乏灵活性与稳定性的问题，难以应对多目标平衡与奖励模型的尺度敏感性。本文旨在拓展对齐空间，提出一种可动态配置、鲁棒性强且高效的新框架。

Method: 提出Swiss-Knife框架，包含可热插拔的评分刀片、候选-批量归一化（CBN）、满足六条公理的成对聚合算子（具有统一数学形式），以及灵活的选择规则。通过理论分析证明成对聚合的稳定性，并结合实验验证其性能优势。

Result: 所提框架在帮助性、诚实性、无害性三者平衡上达到最优，调和F1为0.797，显著优于最强基线（0.750，p < 1e-14）；拒绝率最低，重配置时间仅0.05毫秒，无需梯度计算；消融实验显示CBN至关重要，缺失导致性能下降0.217 F1，且影响力衰减符合预测规律。

Conclusion: Swiss-Knife提供了一个通用、稳定、高效的解码时多目标对齐框架，其成对聚合机制具备理论保障，可有效应对奖励模型的尺度敏感性与对抗性干扰，同时实现快速重配置与卓越性能表现。

Abstract: Decode-time alignment methods steer a frozen language model by scoring candidate continuations with an external reward and selecting the maximiser. We argue that this shared design is a single degenerate point in a much larger space. We introduce Swiss-Knife, a framework for externalised multi-objective alignment in which the alignment specification is a first-class runtime object: hot-swappable scoring blades, a batch normaliser, a pairwise aggregation operator, and a selection rule. Six axioms characterise the admissible aggregation operators, and we prove a representation theorem: every operator satisfying them has the form $R_i = \sum_{j \neq i} g((μ_i - μ_j)/s(σ_i,σ_j))$, a two-parameter family containing probit and logistic comparison rules and pointwise argmax as named coordinates. Within it, pairwise aggregation is Lipschitz-stable under adversarial reward contamination while argmax is not, and Candidate-Batch Normalization (CBN) makes the weight simplex invariant to the rescalings under which reward models are only ever identified. Our reference instantiation pairs DPO-LoRA blades with an uncertainty-aware pairwise tournament. Sweeping the helpfulness/honesty/harmlessness simplex, it attains the best balanced frontier of six decode-time methods (harmonic $F_1$ 0.797 vs. 0.750 for the strongest baseline, $p < 10^{-14}$) with the lowest refusal rate and highest helpfulness of any arm, and reconfigures its objectives in 0.05 ms with no gradient computation. Ablating CBN costs 0.217 $F_1$, and the metadata confirms the predicted mechanism: the lowest-variance blade retains 9% of its nominal 33% influence, and the collapse follows the predicted ordering.

</details>


### [106] [Assessing Adversarial Robustness of Latent Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22228)
*Shaolong Chen,Ang Li,Mingjie Li,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了潜在推理模型（LRMs）在文本和多模态场景下的对抗鲁棒性，发现其普遍低于显式链式思维（CoT）基线，尤其在白盒攻击下表现更差。分析揭示了不同模态的失效模式：文本潜变量对特定输入模式敏感且动态脆弱，而多模态潜变量则对扰动不敏感且对最终预测影响有限。研究指出当前潜在推理方法存在鲁棒性缺陷，强调设计隐式推理系统时需兼顾效率与鲁棒性。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索潜在推理模型（LRMs）在复杂推理任务中的对抗鲁棒性，因其在提升效率的同时可能牺牲安全性，亟需评估其在对抗攻击下的表现。

Method: 在八个模型和六个基准上系统评估文本与多模态场景下的对抗鲁棒性，涵盖白盒与黑盒攻击，分析潜变量动态与对预测的影响。

Result: LRMs在对抗扰动下整体鲁棒性较差，尤其在白盒攻击中性能显著下降；文本潜变量表现出高敏感性和脆性动态，多模态潜变量则对输入扰动不敏感且难以影响输出决策。

Conclusion: 当前潜在推理方法存在显著鲁棒性缺陷，未来设计隐式推理系统时必须同时考虑效率与鲁棒性，避免以牺牲安全性换取计算效率。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on long chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories for complex reasoning, but autoregressive generation brings substantial memory and inference costs. Latent reasoning models (LRMs) offer a more efficient alternative by compressing intermediate reasoning into a small number of continuous latent vectors. Despite their efficiency, however, the adversarial robustness of LRMs remains largely underexplored. In this work, we systematically evaluate the robustness of latent reasoning across textual and multimodal settings, covering eight models and six benchmarks. We find that, across our evaluated settings, LRMs are generally less robust than explicit CoT baselines under adversarial perturbations, with particularly severe degradation under white-box attacks. Further analysis reveals distinct failure modes across modalities: textual latent states exhibit brittle dynamics and high sensitivity to specific input patterns, while latent states in multimodal models can remain largely invariant to input perturbations and have limited influence on final predictions. These findings expose robustness limitations of current latent reasoning approaches and highlight the need to jointly consider efficiency and robustness when designing implicit reasoning systems. We have open-sourced our code to facilitate reproduction of our research https://github.com/PKU-ML/latent-reasoning-model-assessment.

</details>


### [107] [EvalMem: An Operation-Level Diagnostic Framework for Long-Term Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22231)
*Zeyu Liu,Jian Zhong,Rongduo Han,Ziyang Wu,Shunye Tang,Chenghao He,Yaxuan Yang,Yihang Qiu,Ailing Wang,Xiao Liang,Guohuan Xie,Xiaokang Xue,Gongchen Li,Haining Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EvalMem诊断框架，通过三个并行检查员（编码、检索、生成）对长时交互中记忆系统的错误进行细粒度定位；改进检索策略提升召回率，发现检索是主要故障层；基于诊断结果构建辅助结构MemWiki，显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估无法区分长时交互中记忆系统错误的来源（编码、检索或生成），缺乏细粒度诊断能力，限制了系统优化。

Method: 设计EvalMem框架，包含编码、检索、生成三个并行检查员，生成多标签缺陷代码；采用查询与源证据联合搜索的代理RAG策略提升检索召回率；基于诊断结果构建搜索友好的辅助记忆结构MemWiki。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S和DynaMem-Bench上评估显示，检索是主要故障层（缺陷率达22.1%），优于编码（7.7%）和生成（6.5%）；使用MemWiki后，两个基准上的平均准确率分别提升2.5和2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: EvalMem有效识别记忆系统中的缺陷层级，指导针对性优化；所提出的检索增强策略与辅助结构显著提升系统性能，为长时交互中的记忆管理提供可解释性解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactions with LLM-based assistants require memory systems that preserve and update user states, preferences, and interaction histories. Existing evaluations report end-to-end QA accuracy and cannot determine whether errors arise from encoding, retrieval, or generation. We introduce EvalMem, an operation-level diagnostic framework with three parallel Examiners. For each query, the Encoding Examiner checks whether the target fact is stored, the Retrieval Examiner assesses whether the native retriever returns usable evidence, and the Generation Examiner tests whether the model can answer from oracle evidence. Their outputs form fine-grained multi-label defect codes. To improve store-level diagnosis, we adapt agentic RAG with a recall-first strategy that searches using both the query and source evidence, increasing recall of present evidence on LoCoMo from 70.2% to 95.6%. Evaluations of seven memory systems on LoCoMo, LongMemEval-S, and dynamic DynaMem-Bench identify retrieval as the most frequently attributed failure layer; in default LoCoMo, retrieval defects reach 22.1%, compared with 7.7% for encoding and 6.5% for generation. Guided by this diagnosis, MemWiki, a search-friendly auxiliary structure built from each system's memory export, improves mean accuracy by 2.5 and 2.3 percentage points on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S, respectively.

</details>


### [108] [Seeing Through Conflicts: Improving Instruction Hierarchy Alignment in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22234)
*Nicholas Sansoterra,Zishuo Zheng,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLMs）中指令层次（IH）对齐问题，提出通过强化学习与基于规则的奖励训练模型以应对跨模态指令冲突。实验表明，仅使用文本监督的训练效果有限，而图像监督和混合模态监督能显著提升模型在复杂场景下的鲁棒性，尤其在真实图像和网络代理任务中表现良好，且不损害模型的通用多模态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型面临指令嵌入图像、跨模态分裂、视觉变换及代理任务中的交互式冲突等挑战，现有文本主导的指令对齐方法难以有效应对这些多模态复杂情况，亟需更鲁棒的对齐机制。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，设计基于规则的奖励函数，对比文本仅监督、图像仅监督和混合模态监督三种训练方式，评估模型在对抗性、跨模态和交互式指令冲突下的表现。

Result: 混合模态监督训练效果最佳，显著优于纯文本或纯图像监督；其优势可泛化至真实图像和网页代理安全任务，同时保持良好的多模态通用能力。

Conclusion: 轻量级、可验证的多模态监督能有效提升视觉语言模型在复杂指令冲突下的鲁棒性，为构建更安全、可靠的多模态智能系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Instruction hierarchy (IH) alignment teaches language models to prioritize higher-level instructions when inputs conflict. While studied primarily in text-only settings, vision-language models (VLMs) introduce new challenges for IH: instructions may be embedded in images, split across modalities, visually transformed, or encountered during agentic tasks. Positing multimodal IH alignment as a reasoning problem, we train VLMs using reinforcement learning with rule-based rewards, comparing text-only, image-only, and mixed-modality supervision. We find that text-only IH training partially transfers to multimodal attacks, failing when models must decode, reconstruct, or reason over instructions across modalities. Image-based training improves robustness beyond text-only supervision, while mixed-modality training performs best overall. Importantly, the benefits generalize beyond the synthetic typographic training setting to real-image and web-agent safety tasks, while largely preserving general multimodal capability, showing that lightweight, verifiable supervision can meaningfully improve VLM robustness under adversarial, cross-modal, and interactive instruction conflicts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [PRQuant: Permutation Residual Quantization for Low-Overhead Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22106)
*Peiran Wang,Anqi Wang,Jiaying Zhao,Huiwen Yang,Zhenyu Ming,Rongqian Wang,Yiwu Yao,Kun Tian,Xin Yao,Gong Zhang,Fan Yang,Zhongyi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRQuant是一种无需训练、低开销的量化框架，通过通道重排与静态权重残差补偿结合，有效降低线性层低比特量化中的误差。其核心思想是识别对量化误差贡献大的输入通道，将其重新排列至连续尾部块，并离线构建残差子张量。推理时，利用连续布局避免动态收集开销，将分散的残差补偿转为常规的尾部增强GEMM操作，显著降低延迟。实验表明，PRQuant在多个下游任务中优于MXFP4和现有PTQ基线，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507上分别提升1.24和0.55点平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特量化方法（如平滑、旋转、基于残差的方法）虽能缓解异常值问题，但常引入新的权重精度瓶颈，且多为在线实现，导致执行开销大。需要一种无需训练、低延迟、硬件友好的量化方案。

Method: 提出PRQuant（Permutation Residual Quantization），结合通道重排与静态权重残差补偿；在AWQ风格缩放后，识别关键通道并将其移至连续尾部块，离线构造残差子张量；推理时利用连续结构实现高效尾部增强矩阵乘法，避免在线收集操作。

Result: PRQuant显著降低下游投影重建误差；在五个基准测试中平均精度优于默认MXFP4及评估的PTQ基线，在Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507和Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507上分别提升1.24和0.55点；消融实验证明平滑与残差补偿是主要增益来源，而通道重排带来稳定边际提升并消除动态收集开销。

Conclusion: PRQuant是一种高效、低开销的训练自由量化框架，通过通道重排与静态残差补偿，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著降低推理延迟，具备良好的硬件兼容性，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Accuracy of Low-bit quantization of linear layers is often dominated by a small number of outliers. Although existing methods, such as smoothing, rotation, or residual-based approaches, may mitigate this problem, they often introduce new accuracy bottlenecks to weights. Besides, most of these techniques are implemented as online approaches, which can result in heavy execution overheads. To address the afore-mentioned issues, We propose PRQuant (Permutation Residual Quantization), a training-free and low-overhead framework that combines channel reorganization with static weight-side residual compensation. After AWQ-style scaling, PRQuant identifies the input channels that contribute most to weight quantization error, permutes them into contiguous tail blocks, and constructs their residual weight sub-tensors offline. During inference, this contiguous structure enables the activation side to use tail blocks seamlessly without the expensive online gathering operation, and turns scattered residual compensation into a regular tail-augmented GEMM, substantially reducing latency. Experiments demonstrate that PRQuant effectively reduces down-projection reconstruction error. Ablation studies confirm that smoothing and residual compensation are the primary drivers of numerical improvement, while permutation provides a consistent marginal numerical benefit and, more importantly, enables a hardware-friendly contiguous layout that eliminates dynamic gathering overhead. Overall, PRQuant outperforms default MXFP4 and the evaluated PTQ baselines in average accuracy across five downstream benchmarks, improving over MXFP4 by 1.24 and 0.55 on Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 and Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507, respectively.

</details>


### [110] [Generalized Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22107)
*Huizi Cui,Zongbo Han,Chenggong Ding,Naichuan Xiao,Jialong Yang,Jingdong Chen,Guangyu Wang,Qinghua Hu,Changqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的多模态融合模型，通过在大规模合成多模态数据集上训练，学习可迁移的多模态相关性模式，从而实现对任意模态组合和预测任务的泛化能力。实验表明，该模型在18个真实世界数据集上表现优异，无需任务特定调整即可媲美专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合模型仅能处理预定义的模态和单一任务，难以快速适应新应用，因此需要一种能够处理任意模态组合和任务的通用模型。

Method: 基于大规模合成多模态数据集的生成训练，利用多样化因果结构模拟真实世界多模态数据的生成过程，构建统一的基础模型以编码可迁移的多模态相关性，并通过上下文示例在推理时激活相应关联。

Result: 在12种模态、11种预测任务、18个真实世界数据集上的实验显示，该模型性能与专用模型相当，且无需任务特定微调。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用多模态基础模型，具备跨模态、跨任务的泛化能力，为多模态学习提供了一个高效、灵活的新范式。

Abstract: Making prediction with multimodal data is widely used in diverse scenarios. Existing multimodal fusion models, once deployed, can only handle predefined modalities (e.g., vision, text and audio) and single tasks, making it difficult to quickly adapt to new downstream applications. Therefore, a natural yet rather aggressive question arises, whether there exists a general multimodal fusion model that can be applied to arbitrary modality combinations and arbitrary prediction tasks. We argue that a unified multimodal fusion model should not depend on specific modalities and instead encode transferable patterns of multimodal correlation. To this end, we propose a simple and effective learning paradigm based on training over the generation of large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse causal structures that formally characterize the generative processes of multimodal data in real world. Building on this framework, we propose the generalized multimodal foundation model, a unified foundation model for generalized multimodal learning. By constructing large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse correlation patterns, our model encodes transferable multimodal correlations during training and activates appropriate associations through in-context examples during inference. Extensive experiments on 18 real-world datasets spanning 12 modalities and 11 prediction tasks demonstrate that our model achieves competitive performance with specialized models without task-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [111] [Correcting Learning-based Perception for Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22108)
*Yan Miao,Hussein Darir,Sayan Mitra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两步策略来纠正基于机器学习（ML）的状态估计，首先通过离线计算分析ML模块状态估计的不确定性，利用感知合约的预像；其次在运行时使用风险启发式方法从不确定估计中选择特定状态以指导控制决策。在不同天气和道路场景下的仿真评估表明，在45个原始感知控制系统（使用Yolo和LaneNet）导致安全违规的场景中，73%的场景通过该方法保持了安全性，仅27%因感知合约预像构建不完全而无法恢复。此外，该方法不会过度保守，平均仅增加2.8%的完成时间，且干预程度轻微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML-based感知在自主系统中至关重要，但其工作边界不明确，错误感知可能导致不安全或过于保守的控制行为。现有方法缺乏对感知不确定性进行有效建模与修正的能力，因此需要一种可保证安全性的感知校正机制。

Method: 提出两步策略：1）离线计算，利用感知合约的预像表征ML模块带来的不确定性；2）运行时，采用风险启发式方法从不确定估计中选取合适状态用于控制决策。

Result: 在45个存在安全违规的自适应巡航控制（ACC）场景中，73%的场景通过该方法成功维持了安全性，仅27%因预像构造不完整无法恢复。平均完成时间仅增加2.8%，且干预温和，未造成过度保守行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的运行时感知校正策略能有效提升基于ML感知系统的安全性，同时保持控制性能，避免过度保守，适用于复杂多变的实际驾驶环境。

Abstract: Learning-enabled perception is important in many autonomous systems. Unlike traditional sensors, the boundary where ML perception does or does not work is poorly characterized. Incorrect perception can lead to unsafe or overtly conservative downstream control actions. In this paper, we propose a two-step strategy for correcting ML-based state estimation. First, an offline computation is used to characterize the uncertainties resulting from the ML module's state estimation, using preimages of perception contracts. Second, at runtime, a risk heuristic is used to choose particular states from the uncertain estimates to drive the control decisions. We perform extensive simulation-based evaluation of this runtime perception correction strategy on different vision-based adaptive cruise controllers (ACC modules), in different weather conditions, and road scenarios. Out of 45 ACC scenarios where the original perception-based control system using Yolo and LaneNet led to safety violations, in 73% of the scenarios, our runtime perception correction preserved safety; our method wouldn't be able to recover 27% of the scenarios where the construction of the preimages of perception contracts is not fully conformant. Further, our runtime perception correction strategy is not overly conservative---on the average only a 2.8% increase in completion time is experienced in the corrected scenarios, with mild interventions.

</details>


### [112] [A Shared Learning Rate Is Not a Neutral Control in Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22109)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了选择性在线策略蒸馏中学习率与选择器之间的纠缠现象：尽管文献通常使用单一学习率进行比较，但实际结果表明不同选择器对学习率变化的敏感度差异显著，导致结论随学习率大幅波动。例如，在GSM8K任务上，教具性选择器在不同学习率下性能差异高达17.7个百分点，而密集监督则相对稳定。进一步分析显示，这种敏感性并非由优化器步长引起，而是源于选择机制本身，并且实时评分比冻结评分加剧了这一问题。在全微调设置下，这种效应更加明显。研究建议未来应报告选择器与学习率组合的完整矩阵，而非仅依赖单一学习率进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在比较选择性蒸馏中的不同选择器时，普遍采用单一共享学习率作为控制变量，但该设定实际上并不中立，可能误导结论。本文旨在揭示学习率与选择器之间的隐性耦合关系，即‘选择器-学习率纠缠’，并澄清其根本原因。

Method: 通过在GSM8K和MATH-500数据集上使用LoRA微调（Qwen2.5-1.5B学生模型，7B教师模型），系统地在8倍学习率网格下评估多种选择器（如随机子集、总变差选择器、教具性选择器）的表现；引入预注册的冻结评分消融实验（初始学生模型评分，保持预算与滚动策略不变），以分离实时反馈的影响；同时考察全微调场景下的表现变化。

Result: 在GSM8K上，选择性蒸馏的性能随学习率剧烈波动，教具性选择器在1e-4学习率下比密集监督领先10.1个百分点，而在5e-5下仅领先5.1个百分点（2.0倍差异）。冻结评分组仍存在显著率敏感性（p=0.015），说明该现象源于选择机制本身而非反馈循环。全微调下，密集监督波动达19.8 pp，选择性臂达49.5 pp，结论从不显著到显著（+34 pp, p=0.005）转变。在MATH-500上，该现象在LoRA下未复现，但选择性训练约有10个百分点的成本。

Conclusion: 学习率与选择器之间存在深层纠缠，当前文献采用单一学习率进行比较的做法具有误导性。为确保公平比较，必须报告选择器与学习率的完整矩阵，避免将学习率视为无关背景参数。

Abstract: Selective on-policy distillation trains a student only at the token positions a selector scores highest, and the literature compares selectors under a single shared learning rate--a control chosen to be neutral. We show it is not. Under LoRA on GSM8K (Qwen2.5-1.5B student, 7B teacher), across an 8x learning-rate grid, dense supervision is statistically flat (swing 1.8 pp, p=0.26) while every selective arm moves with the rate: 5.4 pp for a random 5% subset, 6.7 pp for a total-variation selector, up to 17.7 pp for a teachability selector. Consequently the dense-versus-selective verdict reads 10.1 pp at lr=1e-4 but 5.1 pp at 5e-5--a 2.0x difference decided by a parameter the protocol treats as scenery--and two of six pairwise significance calls between selectors flip between adjacent rates without any rank inversion. We call this selector-rate entanglement and trace it to selection itself rather than step size: AdamW update magnitudes track the rate to within 2.2% despite 15.5x gradient-norm differences across arms. A preregistered frozen-scoring ablation (selection scored by the initial student; criterion, budget, and on-policy rollouts unchanged; 12 seeds per cell) shows live scoring adds 3.79+/-1.69 pp of rate sensitivity (p=0.035) while the frozen arm remains significantly entangled (p=0.015): the feedback loop aggravates the phenomenon rather than causing it. Under full fine-tuning at the rates this literature actually uses (1e-6 to 1e-5) the pattern grows: dense itself swings 19.8 pp, the selective arm 49.5 pp, and the verdict ranges from a non-significant +3.6 pp at the published operating point to +34 pp (p=0.005) one notch hotter. On MATH-500 the rate dependence does not reproduce under LoRA, scoping that result, while the ~10 pp cost of selective training does. We prescribe reporting the arm x rate matrix, not a shared-rate column, as a precondition for selector comparisons.

</details>


### [113] [Toward Fairness in Machine Learning Models for Predicting Treatment Retention and Premature Discontinuation in Medication for Opioid Use Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22113)
*Tongnian Wang,Carolina Vivas-Valencia,Cici Bauer,Yanmin Gong,Kim-Kwang Raymond Choo,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了用于预测阿片类药物使用障碍（MOUD）治疗保留和提前中断的机器学习（ML）模型的算法公平性，并探讨了偏差缓解技术的有效性。基于2015至2019年美国治疗期数据集-出院数据（TEDS-D），研究训练了四种ML模型，以预测门诊MOUD患者提前中断或超过180天保留的情况。研究评估了整体性能及按种族、族裔、年龄和性别划分的子群体层面的错误率，并结合模型解释分析进行深入探究。结果表明，即使整体预测性能良好，这些模型在不同子群体中仍可能存在显著性能差异；尽管偏差缓解技术可减少但无法完全消除这些差距，且常伴随预测性能的权衡。研究强调了在医疗决策支持中进行公平性感知评估和透明报告子群体表现的重要性，为在MOUD治疗环境中负责任地使用ML模型提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续的低保留率和完成率促使使用机器学习模型预测MOUD治疗保留情况并识别高风险患者。然而，这些模型在不同患者群体中的公平性尚未充分探索，可能影响其在临床决策中的应用效果。因此，亟需系统评估模型的公平性及其对不同人群的影响。

Method: 基于跨年度的治疗期数据集-出院数据（TEDS-D），研究选取2015至2019年间美国门诊接受MOUD治疗的患者数据，训练四种机器学习模型以预测提前中断或超过180天保留的结果。通过评估整体性能与各子群体（按种族、族裔、年龄、性别划分）的误差率，结合模型解释技术分析偏差来源。进一步测试多种偏差缓解策略，评估其对公平性和预测性能的影响。

Result: ML模型在预测MOUD保留与提前中断时，在不同子群体中表现出显著的性能差异，即使整体性能良好。偏差缓解技术虽能降低部分不公平性，但无法完全消除，且常导致预测性能下降。研究揭示了公平性评估的重要性以及透明报告子群体表现的必要性。

Conclusion: 本研究强调在开发和部署用于MOUD治疗风险分层的机器学习模型时，必须纳入公平性考量。应采用公平性感知的评估框架，并公开报告子群体表现，以确保模型在真实世界应用中的责任性与有效性。

Abstract: Persistent low retention and completion rates in medications for opioid use disorder (MOUD) have driven the use of machine learning (ML) models to predict retention and identify patients at risk of premature discontinuation. However, the fairness of these models across patient populations remains largely unexplored, raising concerns about their application in treatment decision support. This study systematically assesses algorithmic fairness in ML models for predicting MOUD retention and premature discontinuation and investigates the effectiveness of bias mitigation techniques. Using the cross-sectional Treatment Episode Data Set-Discharges (TEDS-D), which includes treatment episodes for individuals in the U.S. discharged between 2015 and 2019, we trained four ML models to predict premature treatment discontinuation and retention beyond 180 days among individuals receiving outpatient MOUD. We evaluated overall performance and subgroup-level error rates across patient subgroups defined by race, ethnicity, age, and sex, complemented by model explanation analyses. We further assessed bias mitigation techniques and their effects on both fairness and predictive performance. Our findings demonstrate that ML models for MOUD outcome prediction can exhibit subgroup-level performance gaps even when overall predictive performance appears acceptable and that bias mitigation can reduce, but not fully eliminate, these gaps without trade-offs. By demonstrating the importance of fairness-aware evaluation and transparent reporting of subgroup performance, this study provides practical insights for the responsible and context-sensitive use of ML models for risk stratification and care prioritization in MOUD treatment settings.

</details>


### [114] [ZoAQ: Adaptive Zeroth-Order Querying via Query-Reuse Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22115)
*Yangyang Feng,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZoAQ 是一种基于查询复用的自适应零阶优化方法，通过保留历史评估结果以支持后续更新和决策，实现无需额外验证查询的自适应查询分配。在合成目标上，相比固定预算基线减少43-48%的查询量；在黑盒攻击中，于MNIST和CIFAR-10上分别仅需320和625次平均查询即可达到100%成功率；在四种OPT微调场景中，相较固定K=4节省43-46%前向评估，且任务内精度变化在-0.018至+0.010之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零阶优化中，固定预算策略在每一步使用相同数量的查询，而自适应控制器虽可节省查询但常因额外验证查询而抵消优势。如何在不增加额外开销的前提下实现高效的自适应查询分配，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出ZoAQ方法，利用历史查询结果进行双重用途：既用于生成下一次更新，又用于判断是否需要进一步查询。通过分析历史与当前更新的一致性来指导自适应预算分配，从而避免额外的可靠性测试查询。

Result: 在合成函数上，查询量减少43-48%；在黑盒攻击中，平均查询数分别为320（MNIST）和625（CIFAR-10），成功率100%；在OPT微调任务中，前向评估减少43-46%，精度波动极小（-0.018至+0.010）。

Conclusion: ZoAQ通过查询复用实现了高效、无额外开销的自适应零阶优化，在多种任务中显著降低查询成本，同时保持高精度与稳定性，为实际应用提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Zeroth-order optimization (ZOO) estimates updates from function evaluations, making perturbation queries a primary cost. Fixed budgets spend the same number of queries at every step, while adaptive controllers may offset their savings by using additional oracle calls to test estimator reliability. We introduce ZoAQ, an adaptive ZOO method built around query reuse. Rather than discarding past evaluations after each step, ZoAQ makes them useful for both the next update and the decision to query further. This enables adaptive query allocation without extra validation queries. Our analysis characterizes when this agreement identifies an update that supports descent and guides the controller to a sufficient query budget. On synthetic objectives, ZoAQ reduces queries by 43-48% relative to fixed baselines using 1.2M queries. In black-box attacks, it reaches 100% success with 320 and 625 average queries on MNIST and CIFAR-10, respectively. Across four OPT fine-tuning settings, ZoAQ saves 43-46% forward evaluations relative to fixed K=4, with accuracy changes within tasks ranging from -0.018 to +0.010.

</details>


### [115] [LE4Mob: Towards Inductive, Distance-Aware and General-Purpose Location Embedding for Human Mobility Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22117)
*Xinglei Wang,Stephen Law,Zichao Zeng,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LE4Mob框架，一种基于地理信息的可归纳、距离感知的位置嵌入方法，通过对比语言-位置预训练和距离感知正则化，实现对未见位置的编码，并在多个数据集上优于基线模型，尤其在归纳设置和依赖位置嵌入交互的任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置嵌入依赖移动观测，难以表示未见位置，且对地理距离约束弱，限制了跨数据集和任务的复用性。

Method: 结合对比语言-位置预训练与距离感知正则化，利用地理上下文进行预训练，生成具备空间语义信息的可归纳嵌入。

Result: 在个体级下一次位置预测和群体级通勤流生成任务中，LE4Mob在多个数据集和区域上均优于基线模型，尤其在归纳场景和依赖位置交互的任务中优势明显。

Conclusion: 距离感知、地理衍生的位置表示具有作为人类移动建模可复用基础的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Location representations provide mobility models with fundamental information about the spatial position, functional characteristics, and relationships of places. However, existing embeddings are often dependent on mobility observations, unable to represent unseen locations, and weakly constrained to retain geographic distance. This limits their reuse across datasets and mobility tasks. To address these limitations, we propose LE4Mob, an inductive, distance-aware, and geography-derived location embedding framework for mobility modelling. LE4Mob extends contrastive language-location pre-training while introducing a distance-aware regularisation objective that encourages the embedding space to preserve spatial relationships. Pre-trained from geographic context, LE4Mob can encode rich spatial-semantic information and generate embeddings for unseen locations inductively. Its independence from downstream mobility task supervision also makes it transferable across different mobility tasks. We evaluate LE4Mob on individual-level next location prediction and population-level commuter flow generation. Experiments across multiple datasets and study areas show that LE4Mob outperforms strong baselines, with particular advantages in inductive settings and when downstream models rely directly on interactions between location embeddings. These findings demonstrate the potential of distance-aware, geography-derived location representations as reusable foundations for human mobility modelling.

</details>


### [116] [Success Leaves Detours: Learning Executable Walkthroughs for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22120)
*Kaijie Chen,Chenyu Fang,Liang Yan,Bo Li,Bo Zhang,Peng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Trace框架，通过信用引导和依赖约束，从稀疏奖励轨迹中提取可执行的、状态条件化的操作流程（Walkthrough Memory），有效解决失败、循环和偏离路径问题，显著提升任务完成率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在稀疏奖励环境下难以有效利用历史经验，传统摘要忽略状态条件和动作依赖，导致无法执行；而完整轨迹噪声大、冗余多，限制了复用性。因此需要一种能提取紧凑、可验证、带状态条件的动作序列的方法。

Method: Trace框架包括：1）通过奖励和持久状态变化识别进展锚点；2）传播信用以定位有价值的状态转移；3）基于跨回合成功与失败证据估计动作前置条件；4）反向依赖切片追溯所需事实的产生者，生成依赖一致的动作链，剔除无关循环与绕行。

Result: 在J-TTL、WebShop、ScienceWorld三个基准上，使用三种开源大模型测试，Trace优于8个基线方法，平均AUC提升30.0%，最终-3得分提升40.5%，且推理令牌更少，证明状态条件化可执行流程对长时程任务更具价值。

Conclusion: 长时程交互任务从状态条件化的可执行程序中获益远超完整轨迹或抽象摘要，Trace框架为高效、可靠的任务复用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Test-time self-evolving agents improve by reusing past experience, yet sparse-reward trajectories contain failures, loops, and detours, while summaries often omit the state conditions and action dependencies needed for execution. We study executable Walkthrough induction from sparse-reward trajectories: extracting compact, state-conditioned, and verifiable procedures. Our key observation is that delayed credit identifies actions associated with progress but cannot determine whether they produce facts required by later actions. We propose Trace, a credit-guided, dependency-grounded framework that compiles noisy trajectories into executable Walkthrough Memory. It detects progress anchors from rewards and persistent state changes, propagates credit to identify valuable transitions, and estimates action prerequisites from cross-episode success and failure evidence. Backward dependency slicing then traces required facts to their producers, extracting dependency-consistent action chains while removing irrelevant loops and detours. The resulting Walkthroughs encode entry conditions, ordered state--action--effect steps, and completion and failure predicates, supporting reuse, intermediate-state resumption, and programmatic verification. Experiments on J-TTL, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three open-source LLMs show that Trace consistently outperforms eight test-time learning and memory baselines. Compared with the strongest baseline, it improves average AUC and Final-$3$ by $30.0%$ and $40.5%$, respectively, while using fewer inference tokens. These results show that long-horizon interaction benefits more from state-conditioned executable procedures than from complete trajectories or abstract summaries.

</details>


### [117] [Modelling daily activity patterns from mobile phone location data via deep representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22121)
*Xinglei Wang,Junyuan Liu,Guangsheng Dong,Zichao Zeng,Stephen Law,James Haworth,Tao Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种自监督模型Activity Chain Encoder (ACE)，用于从被动收集的手机位置数据中挖掘人类活动模式。ACE结合城市嵌入、访问时间和持续时间，利用Transformer建模停留序列，通过掩码活动建模和身份引导的对比学习进行训练，无需明确的目的地标签。模型生成的日级表征聚合为用户级画像，并通过聚类与解释，识别出伦敦数据中的六类差异化工作日活动模式，具有不同的日常节奏、城市功能背景和人口统计关联。该方法为从无标签数据中提取行为有意义的人群模式提供了整体路径，源代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手机位置数据虽能反映大规模人类移动，但缺乏直接的活动目的信息。在混合用途城市环境中，访问地点的功能特征与活动目的之间的关系尚不明确，亟需一种无需标签即可挖掘活动模式的方法。

Method: 提出Activity Chain Encoder (ACE)模型，采用自监督学习框架，结合预训练城市嵌入、访问时间与持续时间，使用Transformer建模活动序列；通过掩码活动预测和身份引导的对比学习进行训练；最终将日级表征聚合为用户画像，进行聚类与基于时空-功能模式及人口普查数据的解释。

Result: 在伦敦手机应用位置数据上，ACE成功识别出六类具有显著差异的星期一至星期五活动模式，每类均表现出独特的日常节奏、城市功能背景和人口统计特征，支持构建基于实证的活动模式人物画像。

Conclusion: ACE提供了一种从无标签手机位置数据中提取行为意义人群模式的有效方法，实现了表示、聚类与解释的整合流程，推动了基于真实数据的活动行为研究发展。

Abstract: Passively collected mobile phone location data provide large-scale, longitudinal observations of human mobility but do not directly reveal activity purposes. The functional characteristics of visited locations offer useful contextual information, yet their relationship with activity purpose remains uncertain, particularly in mixed-use urban environments. We conceptualise activity pattern mining as an integrated process of representation, clustering, and interpretation, and propose the Activity Chain Encoder (ACE) for the representation stage. ACE is a self-supervised model that combines pre-trained urban embeddings with visit timing and duration and uses a Transformer to model the sequential organisation of stays. It is trained using masked activity modelling and identity-guided contrastive learning without requiring deterministic activity purpose labels. Learned daily representations are aggregated into user-level profiles, clustered, and interpreted through temporal-functional patterns and Census-derived demographic context. Applied to mobile phone app location data from London and compared with three representative methods, ACE supports the identification of six differentiated weekday activity-pattern groups characterised by distinct daily rhythms, urban functional contexts, and demographic associations. These complementary forms of evidence further support the development of empirically grounded activity-pattern personas, establishing a holistic route for deriving behaviourally meaningful population patterns from unlabelled mobile phone location data. The source code for the entire analytical pipeline developed in this study is publicly available at https://github.com/xlwang233/ACE.

</details>


### [118] [Rank Portability Does Not Imply Feasibility Portability: Target-Specific Evaluation of Joint Hardware Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22122)
*Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨设备硬件评估中架构排名迁移的假设，发现尽管延迟排名在设备间有一定相关性，但可行性集的重叠不完整，且代理设备在支持目标模型选择方面存在显著风险。通过AdaProxy诊断和有限样本分割-共形分析，揭示了证据瓶颈和决策支持的局限性。在10,000个GPT架构上的实验表明，即使延迟相关性很高，代理重用失败的风险仍高达33.3%至100%，说明排名可迁移性、可行性可迁移性和目标特定决策支持是独立的评估维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨设备硬件评估常假设架构排名可迁移，从而使用代理设备进行目标设备的模型选择，但该假设是否成立需验证。特别是联合延迟-能耗可行性是否能在不同设备间保持一致，直接影响模型部署的有效性。

Method: 采用NAS-Bench-201和HW-GPT-Bench数据集，在多个硬件设备上测试架构排名的跨设备相关性；应用AdaProxy诊断改进延迟排名；通过有限样本分割-共形分析评估置信阈值所需的校准样本量；在10,000个GPT架构上进行大规模跨设备验证。

Result: 跨设备排名相关性仅为中等，目标设备可行集重叠不完整；即使延迟排名高度相关（SRCC 0.951–0.996），代理重用失败风险仍高达33.3%至100%；有限样本分析显示至少需要9个校准样本才能达到90%单侧置信度。

Conclusion: 排名可迁移性、可行性可迁移性和目标特定决策支持是三个独立的评估目标，不能相互替代。跨设备评估应明确报告所声称操作点在目标环境中的实际支持情况。

Abstract: Cross-device hardware evaluation often assumes that if architecture rankings transfer across devices, a proxy device can support target-side model selection. We stress-test this assumption for joint latency-energy feasibility across two public architecture families. On NAS-Bench-201, cross-device rank correlations are moderate, while target-comparable feasible-set overlap remains incomplete. A faithful AdaProxy diagnostic substantially improves latency ranking, showing that the observed boundary failures are not simply due to weak adaptation. Exact finite-sample split-conformal analysis also exposes an evidence bottleneck: a finite one-sided 90% threshold requires at least nine calibration observations. We then replicate the phenomenon on 10,000 GPT architectures across 13 HW-GPT-Bench devices. Relative to an RTX3080 proxy, target latency SRCC ranges from 0.951 to 0.996, yet proxy-reuse violation risk ranges from 33.3% to 100% under matched joint constraints. These results show that rank portability, feasibility portability, and target-specific decision support are distinct evaluation objects. Cross-device evaluations should therefore report which target environments actually support the operating point being claimed.

</details>


### [119] [StationPDE: Station-Oriented Surface PDE Learning for Multi-Station Multivariate Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22123)
*Xiao Wang,Changjian Chen,Rongwen Li,Hongwu Liu,Kun Fang,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StationPDE 是一种面向站点的表面偏微分方程（PDE）学习模型，旨在解决多站多变量天气预报中缺乏显式物理演化的问题。它通过从离散站点观测构建地形感知的连续表面场，将物理演化分解为地表风传输和上层大气推断两部分，结合数据驱动的扩散分支与自适应路由机制，实现更准确的站点级多变量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有站点预报模型虽能学习站点间的统计依赖关系，但缺乏显式的物理演化过程；而基于偏微分方程（PDE）的模型虽具物理可解释性，却依赖连续场和上层大气变量，难以应用于仅有地面站点观测的数据。因此亟需一种既能融合物理机制又能适配离散站点数据的新型模型。

Method: StationPDE 通过构建地形感知的连续表面场，将物理演化分解为：1）地表风传输，用于显式演化可观测变量；2）上层大气推断，利用可学习的水平扩散近似缺失的上层影响。同时引入并行的数据驱动扩散分支捕捉互补运动模式，并通过自适应路由器融合两者输出，实现多变量、多站点预报。

Result: 在 Weather2K 和 MeteoNet 数据集上的实验表明，StationPDE 持续优于当前最优基线模型，平均降低均方误差（MSE）约 9.6%。

Conclusion: StationPDE 成功融合了物理可解释性与数据驱动能力，有效弥补了传统站点模型与连续域物理模型之间的鸿沟，为基于离散站点观测的多变量天气预报提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-station multivariate weather forecasting aims to forecast future weather variables at multiple weather stations from historical surface observations. Existing station forecasting models learn statistical dependencies among discrete stations, but lack explicit physical evolution. Meanwhile, PDE-based weather models provide interpretable physical dynamics, yet require continuous fields and upper-air variables unavailable in surface station data. To bridge this gap, we propose StationPDE, a station-oriented surface PDE learning model. StationPDE constructs a terrain-aware continuous surface field from discrete station observations and decomposes its physical evolution into surface wind transport and upper-air inference. Surface wind transport explicitly evolves observable weather variables, while upper-air inference uses learnable horizontal diffusion to approximate the missing influence of unavailable upper-air variables. A parallel data-driven diffusion branch captures complementary motion patterns, and an adaptive router integrates the two forecasts for station-level multivariate forecasting. Experiments on Weather2K and MeteoNet show that StationPDE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, reducing MSE by about $9.6\%$ on average compared with the strongest baseline. Code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/hnu-vis/StationPDE.

</details>


### [120] [SolarFlowRefiner: Refinement-Aware Flow Matching for Surface Solar Radiation Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22126)
*Udbhav Srivastava,Antonita Racheal,Yiheng Chen,Runlong Yu,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SolarFlowRefiner，一种用于高分辨率地表太阳辐射（SSR）降尺度的修正感知流匹配框架。通过条件流匹配生成器预测上采样ERA5基线的归一化修正，再通过修正器基于预测状态与目标残差之间的条件进行训练，使修正器暴露于生成器产生的结构化误差中，并通过反向传播联合优化生成与修正过程，显著提升降尺度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理一致的再分析产品空间分辨率过低，无法捕捉局部云驱动的变异性；单阶段预测模型常导致结构过度平滑，后处理修正存在阶段不匹配问题，且生成与修正独立优化，影响最终重建质量。

Method: 设计了一种修正感知的流匹配框架，包括条件流匹配生成器和基于预测状态的修正器，通过联合优化生成与修正过程，增强对复杂空间结构的建模能力。

Result: 在日块化的ERA5-SolarCube基准测试中，相比独立生成和后处理修正，SolarFlowRefiner表现出持续改进效果，验证了其有效性与通用性。

Conclusion: SolarFlowRefiner提供了一种将生成式预测器与迭代校正器耦合的通用策略，显著提升了高分辨率太阳辐射降尺度的精度与结构保真度。

Abstract: High-resolution surface solar radiation (SSR) is important for solar forecasting and grid operation. However, physically consistent reanalysis products are too coarse to resolve localized cloud-driven variability. In this paper, we study a multisource downscaling task that reconstructs high-resolution SolarCube SSR fields from coarse ERA5 radiative variables and co-registered satellite channels. The task is challenging because a single ERA5 grid cell may contain both sunlit and cloud-shadowed regions. As a result, the missing high-resolution correction can be spatially sharp and inherently ambiguous. One-stage predictors often oversmooth these structures. Post-hoc refinement also introduces a stage-wise mismatch: the generator is optimized independently, even though its output determines the refiner's initial state. We introduce SolarFlowRefiner, a refinement-aware flow-matching framework for SSR downscaling. A conditional FlowMatch generator first predicts a normalized correction to an upsampled ERA5 baseline. The refiner is then trained on prediction-conditioned states between the current FlowMatch output and the target residual. This exposes the refiner to the structured errors produced by the generator. The refinement objective is also backpropagated through the FlowMatch sampler, allowing generation and correction to be jointly optimized for the final reconstruction. Experiments on a day-blocked ERA5--SolarCube benchmark show consistent improvements over standalone generation and post-hoc refinement. More broadly, SolarFlowRefiner provides a general strategy for coupling generative predictors with iterative correctors.

</details>


### [121] [Helix-FNO: Spectral-Domain Operator Learning Coupled with a High-Fidelity Mechanistic Model for Fast Surrogate Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22129)
*Jiabao Zhao,Chuwei Wang,Jinxi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Helix-FNO，一种教师-学生架构，结合32状态的机理教师与傅里叶神经算子（FNO）学生，通过谱域学习解算子而非单一解，实现对整个方程族的泛化。利用拉丁超立方与基于不确定性的主动学习构建高保真数据集，覆盖实际中重要的边界和过载工况。该方法在精度、数据效率和推理延迟方面优于物理信息网络与数据驱动的递归代理模型，推理速度比机理模型快三个数量级，达到毫秒级，适用于大规模候选筛选与在线决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机理模拟模型虽能准确描述污水处理过程的物理化学动态，但计算速度慢，难以满足现代决策引擎每5分钟需数千次前向评估的需求；传统代理模型仅学习单个配置下的特定解，泛化能力差，无法适应新的进水水质、控制设置或厂站布局。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构：机理教师生成高保真输入场到解的配对数据，通过拉丁超立方与不确定性驱动的主动学习策略优化数据采样；学生使用傅里叶神经算子（FNO）在频域学习解算子本身，实现对参数化解流形的整体建模，并引入加权蒸馏损失与主动学习准则以提升性能。

Result: Helix-FNO在准确性、数据效率和推理延迟方面均优于对比方法，实现了比机理模型快三个数量级的毫秒级推理速度，成功将复杂系统模拟嵌入实时决策流程。

Conclusion: Helix-FNO通过学习解算子而非具体解，显著提升了代理模型的泛化能力与运行效率，为大规模快速筛选与在线决策支持提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Mechanistic simulation models of full-scale treatment processes remain the only trustworthy, extrapolative description of the underlying physico-chemical dynamics, yet their runtime is far too slow to support the thousands of forward evaluations that a modern decision engine requires at a 5-minute decision cadence. The standard remedy-surrogate modelling-often produces a network that learns a single solution for a single configuration, so it generalises poorly to new influent profiles, control settings or plant layouts. This paper presents Helix-FNO, a teacher-student architecture that couples a thirty-two-state mechanistic teacher with a Fourier neural operator (FNO) student. The teacher supplies a high-fidelity dataset of input-field-to-solution pairs, curated by Latin-hypercube and uncertainty-based active learning to cover the boundary and overload regimes that matter in practice; the student learns, in the spectral domain, the solution operator itself rather than any single solution, thereby moving from learning one instance to learning an entire family of equations. We give the operator formulation, the spectral convolution definition, the weighted distillation loss and the active-learning criterion, and we analyse the approximation error of a truncated Fourier expansion with respect to the smoothness of the parametric solution manifold. An illustrative study compares Helix-FNO against a physics-informed network and a data-driven recurrent surrogate on accuracy, dataset efficiency and inference latency, and places the methods on a speed-accuracy Pareto front. The resulting operator is three orders of magnitude faster than the mechanistic teacher at millisecond inference, which is precisely the capability required for massive candidate screening and online decision support.

</details>


### [122] [Hierarchical Bayesian optimization of an aircraft-based multi-agent system-of-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22130)
*Paul Saves,Thierry Lefebvre,Nathalie Bartoli,Jasper Bussemaker,Nikolaos Kalliatakis,Nabih Naeem,Prajwal Prakasha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层贝叶斯优化框架，利用高斯过程元模型处理系统架构中离散选择、条件依赖和异构设计变量等复杂问题，显著提升搜索效率与鲁棒性，适用于计算预算有限的复杂系统（如多无人机协同灭火系统）的高效探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统优化方法在处理高成本、黑箱式的多物理场仿真时面临计算开销大和评估失败风险，难以应对复杂系统架构设计中的多样性与不确定性，亟需更高效的建模与优化策略。

Method: 采用分层贝叶斯优化结合高斯过程元模型，构建可处理离散决策、条件依赖及异质变量的架构优化框架，支持大规模、结构多样的设计空间探索。

Result: 相比传统代理模型方法，该框架在有限仿真预算下显著提升了搜索效率与鲁棒性；在欧盟COLOSSUS项目支持的多无人机火灾扑救系统中验证了其有效性，展现出良好的可扩展性与实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将分层贝叶斯优化应用于真实世界系统之系统（SoS）问题，为复杂系统架构设计提供了可迁移的高效优化方法，推动了方法论与工程实践的发展。

Abstract: Developing innovative system architectures increasingly relies on advanced modeling and optimization techniques to frame the architecting process and define the corresponding computational problems. For complex System-of-Systems (SoS), high-fidelity multiphysics and multidisciplinary simulations are essential for capturing detailed behaviors. However, their computational expense and the risk of evaluation failures make direct optimization challenging. To overcome these limitations, surrogate-based approaches, like Bayesian optimization, have emerged as effective tools for managing expensive, black-box simulation tasks. This work introduces a hierarchical Bayesian optimization framework that leverages Gaussian process meta-modeling to handle discrete architectural choices, conditional dependencies, and heterogeneous design variables inherent to SoS problems. Results show that the hierarchical formulation improves search efficiency and robustness compared to conventional surrogate-based methods, enabling the exploration of large and structurally diverse design spaces with limited simulation budgets.
  We apply the approach to an aircraft-based multi-agent system for wildfire suppression, a use case developed within the EU-funded COLOSSUS project that illustrates how SoS principles can coordinate heterogeneous aerial platforms with complementary roles, supporting both sustainable mobility and emergency response missions. Our framework provides a scalable methodology for SoS architecting and model exploration, offering transferable insights for applications in aviation, sustainable mobility, and resilience-oriented system design. By combining hierarchical representations with surrogate-based optimization, this work is among the first practical demonstrations of hierarchical Bayesian optimization applied to real-world SoS problems, advancing both methodology and practice.

</details>


### [123] [Weak Ties, Strong Signals: Efficient Training Data Detection in Diffusion LLMs via Independent Token Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22145)
*Hongyao Yu,Tianqu Zhuang,Ziyuan Xu,Hao Fang,Jiaxin Hong,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为独立令牌采样（ITS）的查询高效框架，用于检测扩散型大语言模型（dLLMs）中潜在的敏感数据泄露。由于dLLMs缺乏自回归模型的一阶概率分解特性，现有方法依赖随机掩码获取可计算的逐令牌检测信号，但无法控制被掩码令牌间的依赖关系，导致非负的结构估计误差，进而掩盖细微的记忆信号。作者通过累积条件互信息（CMI）理论分析了这一误差来源，并提出利用注意力机制推导的成对依赖代理来近似CMI感知的选择标准，同时引入多样性促进策略以增强多轮采样中的令牌覆盖度，从而降低依赖引起的近似误差。实验表明，ITS在多个数据集和模型上均显著优于现有基线，尤其在ArXiv数据集上实现0.18的AUC提升，且在有限查询预算下仍保持优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在检测扩散型大语言模型（dLLMs）中的数据记忆时，因依赖随机掩码而无法有效控制被掩码令牌之间的内部依赖关系，导致结构性估计误差，削弱了对细微记忆信号的检测能力。因此需要一种能减少依赖影响、提升检测可靠性的新方法。

Method: 提出独立令牌采样（ITS）框架，利用注意力机制生成的成对依赖代理来近似累积条件互信息（CMI），指导令牌选择；结合多样性促进策略，在多轮采样中提升令牌覆盖范围，从而获得更稳定的逐令牌检测信号。

Result: ITS在多个数据集和模型上均表现出色，相较现有最佳基线，平均提升显著，尤其在ArXiv数据集上实现了0.18的AUC增益；在低查询预算下依然保持强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ITS通过抑制令牌间依赖带来的估计误差，有效提升了对dLLMs中敏感数据泄露的检测能力，为扩散模型的安全评估提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive models, yet they may expose sensitive training data during denoising. Detecting such usage is challenging because dLLMs lack the efficient one-pass probability decomposition of causal architectures. Existing methods rely on random masking to obtain tractable token-wise detection signals under limited query budgets, but fail to control dependencies among masked tokens. We demonstrate that this token-wise approximation introduces a non-negative structural estimation error, which is theoretically characterized by the cumulative conditional mutual information (CMI) among masked tokens and can obscure subtle memorization signals. This insight suggests that reliable detection requires masked token sets with weak internal dependency. To avoid the prohibitive cost of directly estimating CMI over token combinations, we propose \textit{Independent Token Sampling} (ITS), a query-efficient framework that uses an attention-derived pairwise dependency proxy to approximate the CMI-aware selection criterion. ITS further incorporates a diversity-promoting strategy to improve token coverage across sampling rounds, yielding aggregated token-wise signals that are less affected by dependency-induced approximation error. Experiments on multiple datasets show that ITS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across different models and datasets, achieving an AUC improvement of 0.18 on the ArXiv dataset while maintaining strong performance under limited query budgets. The code is available at https://github.com/Chrisqcwx/DLLM-MIA .

</details>


### [124] [GRRR: The Geometry of Reshaping, Rotation, and Routing in Decoder LLM post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22146)
*Jianing Qi,Hao Tang,Zhigang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了后训练如何改变大语言模型（LLM）的权重相对于预训练权重的变化。通过12个包含监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的后训练链，研究者在奇异值分解（SVD）框架下分析每个权重更新，将其分解为三个几何上不同的组成部分：对角线项（重塑奇异值）、非对角线项（旋转预训练输入与输出方向间的耦合）以及零空间项（将信息引导出原始矩阵的非零SVD核心）。在数学评估套件中发现，移除对角线分量通常仍能保留大部分后训练带来的性能提升。结果表明，后训练带来的改进主要源于对预训练路径的重构与扩展，而非显著改变预训练模型的奇异值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解后训练过程中大语言模型权重变化的本质，特别是其对模型性能提升的贡献机制。现有研究多关注训练过程本身，但缺乏对权重更新几何意义的系统性分析。本文旨在揭示后训练是否通过改变奇异值、旋转方向或引入新路径来实现性能提升。

Method: 采用奇异值分解（SVD）框架分析后训练中权重更新的变化。将每个权重更新分解为对角线部分（影响奇异值）、非对角线部分（影响方向耦合）和零空间部分（引入新方向）。通过在多个后训练链（包括SFT和RL）中进行实验，量化各成分对最终性能的贡献。

Result: 在数学评估任务中，移除对角线成分后，模型性能下降较少，说明大部分性能提升来自非对角线和零空间成分。这表明后训练主要通过重构和扩展预训练路径来提升性能，而非显著改变原始奇异值。

Conclusion: 后训练带来的性能提升主要依赖于对预训练模型路径的重新配置和扩展，而不是大幅度调整其奇异值。这一发现有助于更深入理解大语言模型的可塑性与训练机制。

Abstract: We study how post-training changes the weights of Large Language Models (LLMs) relative to their pretrained weights. Across 12 post-training chains with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), we express each weight update in the pretrained matrix's singular value decomposition (SVD) frame. This decomposition separates the changes of three geometrically distinct components: diagonal values, which reshapes singular values; off-diagonal values, which rotates the coupling between pretrained input and output directions; and null-space values, which routes outside the matrix's original nonzero SVD core. On a math evaluation suite, we find that removing the diagonal component usually preserves most of the gains from post-training. These results suggest that post-training gains are carried primarily by reconfiguring and extending pretrained pathways rather than by substantially changing singular values of pre-trained models.

</details>


### [125] [SafeTune: A Unified Faithful Library for Auditing and Repairing Safety Drift in Fine-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22153)
*Pratinav Seth,Saisab Sadhu,Anshul Kaushal,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeTune 是一个开源库，统一了四种处理大语言模型安全漂移的干预范式：事后权重恢复、安全约束微调、基于梯度的遗忘和推理时引导，并提供共享的可解释性、评估和部署工具。它采用配置驱动的工作流，保持各范式独特输入和干预点，模块化注册表支持新方法、基准、评判标准、模型和微调领域，无需重设计整体流程。通过控制对比和金融、医疗部署案例研究，验证了其在刻画安全漂移、评估可行干预措施及支持校准或分层缓解方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应对大语言模型安全漂移的方法分散且不兼容，缺乏统一的实现、生命周期阶段和评估协议，导致难以采用和比较。

Method: 引入 SafeTune 库，集成四种干预范式（事后权重恢复、安全约束微调、基于梯度的遗忘、推理时引导），并提供统一的配置驱动工作流与模块化注册表，支持扩展新方法与评估体系。

Result: 通过控制实验和实际部署案例（金融与医疗），证明 SafeTune 能有效刻画安全漂移、评估干预效果，并支持灵活、校准或分层的安全缓解策略。

Conclusion: SafeTune 为 LLM 安全漂移问题提供了可复用、可扩展、可比较的统一框架，显著提升安全干预方法的实用性与系统性。

Abstract: Methods for addressing safety drift in fine-tuned Large Language Models (LLMs) are scattered across incompatible implementations, lifecycle stages, and evaluation protocols, making them difficult to adopt and compare. We introduce SafeTune, a source-available library that unifies four intervention paradigms: post-hoc weight recovery, safety-constrained fine-tuning, gradient-based unlearning, and inference-time steering, alongside shared interpretability, evaluation, and deployment utilities. SafeTune provides a consistent configuration-driven workflow while preserving the distinct inputs and intervention points each paradigm requires. Its modular registry supports new methods, benchmarks, judges, models, and fine-tuning domains without redesigning the surrounding pipeline. We demonstrate SafeTune through controlled comparisons and finance and medical deployment case studies, showing how it characterizes safety drift, evaluates feasible interventions on common refusal-behavior and capability evaluations, and supports calibrated or layered mitigation.

</details>


### [126] [A Comparative Framework for Evaluating Foundation Models on Tabular Data: A Case Study in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22154)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个名为\system{}的比较评估框架，用于在六个临床相关维度上评分和排名表格基础模型（TFMs），包括泛化能力、隐私保护、数据需求、可扩展性、可解释性和公平性。该框架将每个维度分解为可度量的子组件，并支持组合生成复合指标（超指标），以诊断模型在多个维度上的表现。通过两个医疗应用场景（铁缺乏筛查和心力衰竭预测）展示了框架的实际应用，说明不同临床重点会导致不同的模型排名。同时提供了一个包含45种TFM的分类体系，按其架构组织，便于研究者和从业者导航该快速发展的领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对表格基础模型的综述仅列出其功能，缺乏结构化方法来根据实际应用需求进行比较，导致临床医生或数据科学家难以选择合适的模型。

Method: 设计并实现\system{}框架，涵盖六项临床重要维度，每项维度下设可度量的子组件；支持构建超指标以综合评估多维表现；通过真实医疗场景验证框架有效性。

Result: 在铁缺乏筛查和心力衰竭预测任务中，系统能够根据临床关注点差异得出不同的模型排名，证明其灵活性与实用性；同时提供了覆盖45种模型的架构分类参考体系。

Conclusion: \system{}框架为选择适合特定临床任务的表格基础模型提供了系统化、可操作的决策支持工具，有助于推动高质量医疗人工智能模型的应用落地。

Abstract: Tabular data is the most common format in clinical practice, encompassing laboratory results, medication records, diagnostic codes, and patient demographics. As foundation models for tabular data have grown in number and variety, a practical question has become harder to answer: which model should a clinician or data scientist actually choose for a given task, and why? Existing surveys catalogue what these models can do, but they stop short of providing a structured way to compare them against the specific demands of a real application. We introduce \system{}, a comparative evaluation framework that scores and ranks tabular foundation models (TFMs) across six clinically meaningful dimensions: how well a model generalizes to new datasets, how effectively it protects patient privacy, how much data it needs to perform well, how it scales with growing datasets and feature spaces, how interpretable its predictions are to clinicians, and how fairly it performs across patient subgroups. Each dimension is broken down into measurable sub-components, and groups of sub-components can optionally be combined into supplementary compound scores, called super-metrics, that provide a diagnostic view of how a model performs across several dimensions simultaneously. To show how the framework works in practice, we apply it to two healthcare use cases, screening for iron deficiency and predicting heart failure, demonstrating how the same set of metrics leads to different model rankings depending on what matters most in each clinical context. We also provide a taxonomy of 45 TFMs organized by their underlying architecture, which serves as a reference for researchers and practitioners looking to navigate this rapidly expanding field.

</details>


### [127] [From Latent Biomarkers to Clinical Rules: Embedding-Guided Rule Mining and Attribution-Based Translation for Interpretable Tabular Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22155)
*Majid Lotfian Delouee,Hamed Ayoobi,Sjors G. J. G. In 't Veld,Martijn C. Schut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种四步流程，从FT-Transformer的潜在空间中挖掘决策规则，并将其翻译回可测量的临床特征。该方法利用嵌入维度作为潜在生物标志物，通过小决策树挖掘规则，并使用梯度输入显著性和CLS注意力归因进行翻译。在六个公开的临床和人群健康数据集上评估，翻译后的规则在五个数据集中优于原始特征规则，平均AUROC提升0.04至0.23。但在心脏病数据集中，潜在空间规则的AUROC为0.98，翻译后降至0.72，表明高性能的潜在规则无法总是由简单的原始特征条件表示。结果表明，潜在空间规则发现能够揭示预测模式，并将其转化为临床可评估的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持工具需要准确预测与可理解解释相结合。规则模型虽透明，但直接从原始临床测量中提取的规则可能忽略多变量间的复杂交互模式。因此，需要一种方法在保持预测性能的同时，生成可解释且可临床验证的规则。

Method: 提出四步管道：1）将患者分组并识别在嵌入空间中持续区分群体的嵌入维度作为潜在生物标志物；2）使用小决策树从潜在空间挖掘决策规则；3）利用梯度输入显著性和CLS注意力归因将潜在规则映射回原始临床特征；4）在多个数据集上评估翻译规则的性能。

Result: 在六组数据集中，翻译后的规则在五组中优于原始特征规则，平均AUROC提升0.04至0.23。在心脏病数据集中，潜在空间规则达到0.98 AUROC，翻译后下降至0.72，表明高绩效的潜在规则不能总是被简单原始特征条件表达。

Conclusion: 潜在空间规则发现能够捕捉复杂的预测模式，并通过可解释的临床特征翻译，使结果可用于临床评估，从而增强临床决策支持系统的可信度与实用性。

Abstract: Clinical decision support tools are most useful when accurate predictions are accompanied by understandable explanations. Rule-based models provide transparency, but rules derived directly from raw clinical measurements may miss patterns arising from interactions between multiple variables. We present a four-step pipeline that mines decision rules in the latent space of an FT-Transformer and translates them back into measurable clinical features. Embedding dimensions that consistently separate patient groups are treated as latent biomarkers, rules are mined using small decision trees, and selected rules are translated using gradient-input saliency and CLS attention attribution. We evaluate the framework on six public clinical and population health datasets at four embedding dimensions. Translated rules outperformed raw-feature rules in five of six datasets, with mean AUROC gains ranging from 0.04 to 0.23. On the heart disease dataset, embedding-space rules reached 0.98 AUROC, but translation reduced this to 0.72, showing that high-performing latent rules cannot always be represented by simple raw-feature conditions. These results show that latent-space rule discovery can uncover predictive patterns while translating them into clinically measurable features that can be evaluated by clinicians.

</details>


### [128] [The Limits of Speculation: Bounding Speculative Decoding in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22156)
*Aidar Amankulov,Denis Mamatin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将推测预算选择建模为基于参考序列的离线随机最短路径问题，提出一个使用反事实模拟的诊断Oracle来分析MoE模型中的验证成本。在Qwen3-Coder与EAGLE-3组合上的分析表明，被拒绝的候选者形成严格线性边界，揭示了全局优化在局部上受边际成本与预期进展比（Δ𝔼[Cost]/Δ𝔼[a]）平衡条件的约束，为未来自适应在线启发式设计提供了严格的数学基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决混合专家（MoE）模型中推测解码因输入相关专家负载导致的验证成本不稳定问题，理解其内在机制以支持更高效的推理设计。

Method: 将推测预算选择建模为离线随机最短路径（SSP）问题，构建基于反事实模拟的诊断Oracle，通过在边际增量空间（Delta Space）中分析决策行为，识别被拒绝候选者的结构特性。

Result: 发现被拒绝的候选者在边际增量空间中形成严格的线性边界，表明复杂全局优化在局部上由边际成本与预期进展比的平衡条件主导。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计未来的自适应在线启发式算法提供了坚实的数学理论基础，揭示了推测解码中验证成本调控的核心机制。

Abstract: Speculative decoding in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models faces the problem of unstable verification cost caused by input-dependent expert loading. To study the physics of this process, we formulate speculation-budget selection as an offline Stochastic Shortest Path (SSP) problem over reference sequences and build a diagnostic Oracle that uses counterfactual simulation to account for MoE verification cost. A detailed analysis of the Oracle's decisions on the Qwen3-Coder and EAGLE-3 pairing, in the space of marginal deltas (Delta Space), shows that rejected candidates form a strict linear boundary. This result demonstrates that a complex global optimization is locally governed by a necessary condition balancing marginal cost against expected progress ($\frac{Δ\mathbb{E}[Cost]}{Δ\mathbb{E}[a]}$), providing a rigorous mathematical reference point for designing future adaptive online heuristics.

</details>


### [129] [PAGE: Partition-Aware Gated KV-Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22157)
*Pankaj Kumar,Subhankar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PAGE提出将KV缓存淘汰重视为输入级准入决策，通过预填充阶段的注意力头对齐下降度（early-to-late drop in pairwise top-k head agreement）预测输入的安全性，从而决定是否执行淘汰。该方法无需标签、无需训练，仅用一个标量阈值即可在关键输入上避免精度从99%骤降至0%，显著降低误伤率（从0.75降至0.026），实现29倍的改善。它仅在淘汰危险时介入，其余情况保持透明，压缩率实际为2.9倍（名义16倍），且不牺牲模型性能，是面向安全性的轻量级防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存淘汰方法只决定淘汰哪些token，而不决定是否淘汰，导致某些输入在特定内存预算下可能因淘汰引发灾难性精度下降（如99%→0%）。需要一种能根据输入特性判断淘汰风险的机制，以提升系统鲁棒性。

Method: 引入一个基于预填充阶段注意力头对齐变化的标量指标——早-晚top-k头一致性的下降度，作为输入安全性的代理信号；定义一个门控机制（PAGE），当该下降度较大时启用基础淘汰策略，否则保留完整缓存；该阈值可通过约100个无标签样本进行快速校准。

Result: 在四个模型家族、四种淘汰器、两个基准测试中，PAGE将容量受限类输入的误伤率从0.75降至0.026（29倍改善），将原本崩溃的精度曲线稳定在89%左右，且无需重新训练淘汰器；实际压缩比为2.9倍（名义16倍），对安全输入无影响，是精准的安全保障而非通用压缩方案。

Conclusion: PAGE是一种无需训练、无需标签的输入级动态准入机制，能够有效识别并保护易受淘汰伤害的输入，显著提升系统稳定性，同时维持高压缩效率，是部署中可靠性的关键增强。

Abstract: KV-cache eviction methods decide which tokens to keep but not whether to evict at all, so a benchmark mean can hide a class of inputs on which compression drives accuracy from 99\% to 0\%. We reframe eviction as a per-input admission decision and show that inputs separate into a capacity-bound class, where eviction is catastrophic at every budget, and a dilution-prone class, where eviction is safe or beneficial. A single label-free scalar computed from prefill attention, the early-to-late drop in pairwise top-$k$ head agreement, predicts this class before any decoding. PAGE thresholds this drop: it applies any base evictor when the drop is large and retains the full cache otherwise, with no training and no accuracy labels. The drop orders inputs by eviction safety consistently across four architecture families, and a per-model unlabeled pilot of about 100 inputs recalibrates the threshold for a new family. Used as a safeguard, PAGE cuts the harm rate on the capacity-bound regime from 0.75 to 0.026, a 29 $\times$ reduction, across four evictors, four models, and two benchmarks, turning a 99\% to 0\% collapse into a flat 89\% without retraining the evictor. The gate is inert wherever eviction is already safe, and the capacity-bound class it protects is a small, identifiable minority of inputs, so the benefit is a targeted safety gain rather than an average one. PAGE is a per-input safeguard, not a compressor: realized compression is $1.8 - 3.4 \times$ (mean 2.9$\times$) against a nominal 16$\times$ budget and decays toward unity by batch 16 under static provisioning, and a trained evictor wins at matched memory.

</details>


### [130] [StepKV: Step-Aware KV Cache Compression for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22158)
*Boyu Feng,Jiahong Liu,Yifan Li,Wenhao Yu,Zexuan Qiu,Yuliang Sun,Ming Shen,Xiang Li,Quanyu Dai,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StepKV提出一种新的键值（KV）缓存压缩方法，针对多步大语言模型代理推理中因传统按令牌级别修剪导致的推理连续性中断问题。它将推理步骤视为保留单位，结合轨迹信号估计步骤效用，并融合令牌级显著性进行全局排序，从而在低缓存预算下保持更高的准确率，在多跳问答和长周期网络推理任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法通常将缓存视为扁平的令牌流，按最近性或注意力显著性对令牌进行排序，但这种做法与多步推理中的信息组织方式不匹配，容易造成早期重要信息被误删，导致推理连续性中断。因此需要一种能同时考虑令牌和推理步骤层级信息的压缩机制。

Method: StepKV将生成缓存条目的推理步骤作为第一类保留单元，为每个缓存条目关联其所属的推理步骤，利用轨迹衍生信号估计步骤的实用性，并将该实用度与令牌级别的显著性相结合，生成综合评分以全局排名可压缩的令牌，最终在目标预算内保留得分最高的条目。

Result: 在多跳问答和长周期网络推理任务中，StepKV在低KV缓存预算下仍能维持较高准确性，而传统的令牌级基线方法则迅速下降，展现出更优的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: StepKV通过引入以推理步骤为中心的视角，有效缓解了多步大语言模型代理推理中的KV缓存压缩难题，显著提升了缓存利用效率与推理鲁棒性。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference, but the cache grows linearly with context length, increasing storage and decoding costs. KV cache compression mitigates this cost by retaining only a subset of cached tokens. This challenge is particularly important for multi-step LLM agents, where a query expands into trajectories of reasoning, tool interactions, and retrieved observations. Existing pruning methods typically treat the cache as a flat token stream and rank tokens by recency or attention saliency. This creates a mismatch between the unit of compression and the unit of reasoning: token-level pruning removes individual entries, whereas useful information in multi-step agents is often organized into reasoning steps with uneven and delayed importance. Consequently, an early observation or intermediate decision may receive little recent attention yet remain essential for later evidence synthesis. We term this failure mode Reasoning Continuity Disruption.These observations motivate KV cache compression that jointly considers token- and reasoning-step-level information. StepKV addresses this goal by treating reasoning steps as first-class retention units. It associates cache entries with their generating steps, estimates step utility from trajectory-derived signals, and combines this utility with token-level saliency. The resulting scores globally rank prunable tokens, from which StepKV retains the top-scoring entries under a target budget. StepKV thus provides a step-centric perspective for agent KV cache compression. Across multi-hop QA and long-horizon web reasoning tasks, StepKV sustains accuracy under low KV budgets where token-level baselines degrade sharply, offering a more robust efficiency-accuracy trade-off for multi-step agent inference.

</details>


### [131] [Uncertainty and Business-Aware Remaining Useful Life Estimation for Semiconductor Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22160)
*Davide Frizzo,Francesco Borsatti,Gian Antonio Susto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合深度学习序列模型与同时分位数回归（SQR）的预测性维护（PdM）框架，用于半导体制造中剩余使用寿命（RUL）的不确定性感知估计和风险敏感的维护决策。在2018年PHM数据挑战赛的离子刻蚀数据上对比多种架构，其中基于状态空间模型（SSM）的S4D表现最佳，在各分位数下均提供更优的RUL估计。相比预防性维护基线，该方法显著降低业务成本，避免了过早干预，支持在半导体生产中实现考虑不确定性和成本敏感性的维护规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造依赖高度互联的组件，单个资产故障可能引发整个生产链中断。因此需在意外故障与过早停机之间取得平衡，实现高效维护规划。现有方法缺乏对预测不确定性的有效建模和成本敏感决策支持，亟需一种能够同时评估风险与寿命的先进维护框架。

Method: 采用深度学习序列模型与同时分位数回归（SQR）联合建模，结合状态空间模型（SSM）架构，特别是对角状态空间（S4D），实现对剩余使用寿命（RUL）的多分位数预测，并引入业务指标如意外中断（UB）、未利用寿命（UL）及加权成本目标进行综合评估。通过多模型对比验证性能。

Result: S4D在所有分位数上的RUL估计表现最优；相较于预防性维护基线，显著降低了业务成本，减少了因过早干预导致的资源浪费，提升了维护决策的经济性与可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于S4D与SQR的预测性维护框架能有效实现不确定性感知的RUL估计与成本敏感的维护决策，适用于复杂、高价值的半导体制造环境，为工业系统维护提供了可推广的技术路径。

Abstract: Semiconductor manufacturing relies on tightly interconnected components, so early identification of the assets most likely to fail is essential to prevent a single breakdown from disrupting the entire production pipeline. Maintenance planning must therefore balance unexpected failures against prematurely interrupted operating life. We present a Predictive Maintenance (PdM) framework combining Deep Learning (DL) sequence models and Simoultaneous Quantile Regression (SQR)
  for uncertainty-aware Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimation and risk-aware maintenance decisions. Several architectures are compared on ion-milling data from the 2018 PHM Data Challenge (PHM18), including architectures based on State Space Models (SSM), using prediction and business metrics: Unexpected Breaks (UB), Unexploited Lifetime (UL), and a cost-weighted objective. Diagonal State Spaces (S4D) delivers the best Remaining Useful Life (RUL) estimates across quantiles and, relative to Preventive Maintenance (PvM) baselines, substantially lowers business cost by avoiding systematically early interventions. The results support uncertainty-aware, cost-sensitive maintenance planning in semiconductor production.

</details>


### [132] [TARGet: Topology-Aware Fusion-based Radio Frequency Circuit Functional Modeling using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22165)
*Soroosh Noorzad,Sebastian Bodero,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TARGet提出了一种开源的拓扑感知射频电路建模方法，通过结合图神经网络（GNN）和子电路连接感知神经网络，利用S参数表示子电路行为并显式融入电路拓扑信息，实现了在少量训练数据下的高精度预测。实验表明，TARGet在多个射频电路拓扑上实现小于1%的预测误差，训练数据需求减少最多35.5倍，并在1%误差阈值下预测精度提高9.7倍；零样本迁移测试中，对未见子电路拓扑的预测误差降低达45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法在模拟和射频电路自动合成中面临挑战，包括需为每种电路拓扑单独训练、忽略拓扑信息以及依赖全连接网络导致数据需求量大。这些限制阻碍了模型在不同拓扑间的泛化能力。

Method: TARGet采用双层次建模：在子电路层面使用S参数表示行为以实现可重用的射频构建块抽象；在整体拓扑层面，通过融合图神经网络与连接感知神经网络，显式集成电路拓扑信息，提升数据效率和跨拓扑泛化能力。

Result: TARGet在多个射频电路拓扑上实现亚1%预测误差，训练数据需求减少最多35.5倍，1%误差阈值下预测精度提升9.7倍；零样本迁移测试中NMAE降低高达45%。

Conclusion: TARGet是一种高效、可扩展且具备强泛化能力的拓扑感知射频电路建模方法，显著降低了对训练数据的需求，并在跨拓扑场景下展现出卓越的性能，为自动电路合成提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Automatic synthesis of analog and Radio Frequency (RF) circuits is an emerging area that requires an efficient circuit modeling method. In recent years, Machine Learning (ML) solutions have played a promising role in this regard. However, many existing ML approaches require separate training data for each circuit topology, even when a single circuit component is added or removed. In addition, they overlook circuit topology information, which limits their ability to capture complex component interactions. Furthermore, they rely on fully connected neural networks with flat feature representations, which require substantial amounts of training data. In this work, we propose an open-source topology-aware RF circuit modeling method, TARGet. Our model considers the circuit at two levels: sub-circuits and the overall circuit topology. At the sub-circuit level, TARGet leverages S-parameter representations to capture sub-circuit behavior rather than relying on individual circuit components, providing a reusable behavioral abstraction for RF building blocks. Moreover, TARGet explicitly incorporates circuit topology information into the model, enabling it to learn across multiple topologies. TARGet introduces a novel fusion-based architecture that integrates Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and sub-circuit connectivity-aware neural networks to improve data efficiency. Experimental evaluation across multiple RF circuit topologies demonstrates that TARGet achieves sub-1% prediction error while reducing the required training data by up to 35.5x compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. Furthermore, TARGet achieves 9.7x higher prediction accuracy under a strict 1% error threshold relative to SOTA models. A held-out matching-network evaluation further demonstrates zero-shot transfer to an unseen sub-circuit topology, where TARGet reduces NMAE by up to 45%.

</details>


### [133] [Clustering-Based Collective Anomaly Detection in IoT Systems: A Graph Neural Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22166)
*Dalila Khettaf,Djamel Djenouri,Zeinab Rezaeifar,Youcef Djenouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UGCAD is a novel unsupervised framework for detecting collective anomalies in IoT network traffic by leveraging graph-structured data and a variational graph autoencoder (VGAE) to enhance clustering. It identifies normal clusters, refines them, and applies anomaly scores to detect collective anomalies, outperforming existing methods on CICIoT2023 and ToN-IoT datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The increasing deployment of IoT devices has led to higher network traffic and more cyberattack opportunities, necessitating effective anomaly detection.
